Title: Sixteen Discourses on Jesus Christ our Lord, being an Exposition of the Second Part of the Creed, Preached at Berlin; translated from the High Dutch, second edition
Creator: Nikolaus Ludwig, Count von Zinzendorf (1700-1760); translator unnamed on the title page
Publisher: London: printed by William Bowyer
Publication Date: 1750
Rights: Public Domain (published 1750, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Source: Internet Archive item bim_eighteenth-century_sixteen-discourses-on-je_zinzendorf-nicolaus-lud_1750 (https://archive.org/details/bim_eighteenth-century_sixteen-discourses-on-je_zinzendorf-nicolaus-lud_1750), the item's own bim_eighteenth-century_sixteen-discourses-on-je_zinzendorf-nicolaus-lud_1750_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: Internet Archive; host metadata date: 1750. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: 572b12ea5413aeca37ca21aad17a245a978b62df00e8a9f30b4ba746e424d228.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. British Library microfilm scan; readable throughout with the long s kept; the printer's own preface explains that this second edition was compared again with the German.
Content note: The second series of Berlin discourses, on the second article of the Creed, in the revised English of 1750; each discourse is a chain of short sentences, as the author's own preface says.
